Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The coordination variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 377 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 894 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by coordination generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 377, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 894. QED.
